## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your partner for work on this Mac. For example, I can track down and fix a failing test in one of your repos.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 저장소 작업, 파일 정리, 웹 확인, 계정 메일·일정 확인 같은 일을 도와드릴 수 있어요. 예를 들어 "이 저장소에서 깨진 테스트 고쳐줘"처럼 말씀해 주시면 바로 시작할게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 고마워요. 내일 아침에 봐요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요. 안녕히 주무세요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を重視する通信」、UDPは「速さと軽さを重視する通信」です。TCPは届いたか確認し、順番どおりに再送して届けますが、UDPはそうした確認をせず、届けっぱなしで速く送ります。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **rebase** para ordenar tu rama local o privada antes de compartirla, mientras nadie más la tenga. Usa **merge** para integrar en una rama compartida (como `main`) o cuando ya subiste la rama y otros pueden estar trabajando sobre ella, porque rebase reescribe el historial.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠传输的：先握手建立连接，保证数据有序、不丢不重；UDP 不建连接、不保证可靠，速度快、开销小，但数据可能丢失或乱序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local, unpushed branch to keep its history clean before sharing. Merge when the branch is already shared or others have based work on it, since rebasing rewrites commits they may depend on.
```

Run the test suite and report the actual command and its result (pass/fail counts). If any test fails, report which and why. ⏎ 4. Report the file(s) changed and the old and new values. ⏎  ⏎ Out of scope: do not merge into the base branch, do not push, do not open a PR, do not publish or release. ### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to the database on disk before the change is applied to the main data files. If the system crashes midway, the log is replayed on restart so committed work is not lost and partial work can be rolled back.
```

  .k-row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--d-line,#e5e5e5)}

  function render() {
    var trains = ducter.get('trains') || [];
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    var count = document.getElementById('count');

      var left = t.left ? '余 ' + t.left + ' 张' : '';

        '<div class="k-row-sub">二等座' + (left ? ' · ' + left : '') + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value' + (t.price < 600 ? '' : ' over') + '">¥' + t.price + '</div>';
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    empty.hidden = under.length > 0;
    if (under.length) {
      count.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    } else {
      count.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">没有</span>';
    }
    ducter.summary(under.length ? under.length + ' 班 600 元以下' : '暂无 600 元以下');
  }
  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
</script>

});

});

```
--- step 1: user types in the sheet of their app (the first one listed in [apps]), about it: 加个合计，放在最上面，大一点
  [partner message] 合计加在了最上面,现在是 ¥1,966(3 笔)。下面记一笔就自动加总。
  [an app card under the message: changed "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2432 characters): <main class="k-app">
<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
<details class="k-add">
  <summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what" type="text"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>金额(元)</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" type="text" placeholder="2026-11-26"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
function xs(){return ducter.get("expenses")||[];}
function fmt(n){return "¥"+n.toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const list=xs();
  const sum=list.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=list.length?fmt(sum):"¥0";
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=list.length?list.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  const ul=document.getElementById("list");
  ul.innerHTML="";
  if(!list.length){const e=document.createElement("div");e.className="k-empty";e.textContent="还没有花销,点下面的记一笔";ul.appendChild(e);return;}
  for(const x of list){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent=fmt(Number(x.yuan));
    const wrap=document.createElement("div");wrap.appendChild(main);wrap.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(wrap);row.appendChild(val);ul.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(list.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(sum));
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=Number(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  if(!what||!yuan)return;
  const list=xs().concat([{what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day}]);
  ducter.set("expenses",list);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
  document.getElementById("day").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

  }

  $('add').onclick = async () => {
    const amount = parseFloat($('amt').value.replace(',', '.'));
    if (!(amount > 0)) { $('amt').focus(); return; }
    items.push({
      id: Date.now(),
      amount: Math.round(amount * 100) / 100,
      cat: newCat,
      note: $('note').value.trim(),
      date: today()
    });
    $('amt').value = '';
    $('note').value = '';
    $('addBox').open = false;
    await save();
  };

  ducter.onChange(async () => {
    const v = await ducter.get(KEY);
    items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  });

  (async () => {
    const v = await ducter.get(KEY);
    items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  })();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
